	# Referendum Single State
	focus = {
		id = KAZ_cas_referendum_single_state
		icon = kaz_cas_unity

		x = -1
		y = 8
		relative_position_id = KAZ_cas_central_asian_cooperation

		cost = 9

		prerequisite = { focus = KAZ_cas_confederate_treaty }
		mutually_exclusive = { focus = KAZ_cas_strengthen_confederation }

		available = {
			is_subject = no
			NOT = { has_idea = non_power }
			NOT = { has_idea = minor_power }
			NOT = { has_government = nationalist }
			NOT = { has_government = fascism }
			is_faction_leader = yes
			has_stability > 0.75
			if = {
				limit = {
					UZB = {
						exists = yes
						is_in_faction_with = KAZ
					}
				}
				UZB = {
					OR = {
						is_subject_of = KAZ
						influence_higher_70 = yes
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					KYR = {
						exists = yes
						is_in_faction_with = KAZ
					}
				}
				KYR = {
					OR = {
						is_subject_of = KAZ
						influence_higher_70 = yes
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					TAJ = {
						exists = yes
						is_in_faction_with = KAZ
					}
				}
				TAJ = {
					OR = {
						is_subject_of = KAZ
						influence_higher_70 = yes
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					TRK = {
						exists = yes
						is_in_faction_with = KAZ
					}
				}
				TRK = {
					OR = {
						is_subject_of = KAZ
						influence_higher_70 = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}

		bypass = {
			NOT = { country_exists = UZB }
			NOT = { country_exists = KYR }
			NOT = { country_exists = TAJ }
			NOT = { country_exists = TRK }
		}

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus KAZ_cas_referendum_single_state"
			add_political_power = -300
			add_named_threat = {
				threat = 3
				name = KAZ_cas_confederation_threat
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					country_exists = UZB
					UZB = { is_in_faction_with = KAZ }
				}
				UZB = {
					every_core_state = { add_core_of = KAZ }
					every_unit_leader = { set_nationality = KAZ }
				}
				KAZ = {
					add_to_variable = { treasury = UZB.treasury }
					clamp_variable = { var = treasury min = -1000000 max = 1000000 }
					add_to_variable = { debt = UZB.debt }
					clamp_variable = { var = debt min = 0 max = 1000000 }
					add_to_variable = { int_investments = UZB.int_investments }
					clamp_variable = { var = int_investments min = 0 max = 1000000 }
				}
				annex_country = { target = UZB transfer_troops = yes }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					country_exists = KYR
					KYR = { is_in_faction_with = KAZ }
				}
				KYR = {
					every_core_state = { add_core_of = KAZ }
					every_unit_leader = { set_nationality = KAZ }
				}
				KAZ = {
					add_to_variable = { treasury = KYR.treasury }
					clamp_variable = { var = treasury min = -1000000 max = 1000000 }
					add_to_variable = { debt = KYR.debt }
					clamp_variable = { var = debt min = 0 max = 1000000 }
					add_to_variable = { int_investments = KYR.int_investments }
					clamp_variable = { var = int_investments min = 0 max = 1000000 }
				}
				annex_country = { target = KYR transfer_troops = yes }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					country_exists = TAJ
					TAJ = { is_in_faction_with = KAZ }
				}
				TAJ = {
					every_core_state = { add_core_of = KAZ }
					every_unit_leader = { set_nationality = KAZ }
				}
				KAZ = {
					add_to_variable = { treasury = TAJ.treasury }
					clamp_variable = { var = treasury min = -1000000 max = 1000000 }
					add_to_variable = { debt = TAJ.debt }
					clamp_variable = { var = debt min = 0 max = 1000000 }
					add_to_variable = { int_investments = TAJ.int_investments }
					clamp_variable = { var = int_investments min = 0 max = 1000000 }
				}
				annex_country = { target = TAJ transfer_troops = yes }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					country_exists = TRK
					TRK = { is_in_faction_with = KAZ }
				}
				TRK = {
					every_core_state = { add_core_of = KAZ }
					every_unit_leader = { set_nationality = KAZ }
				}
				KAZ = {
					add_to_variable = { treasury = TRK.treasury }
					clamp_variable = { var = treasury min = -1000000 max = 1000000 }
					add_to_variable = { debt = TRK.debt }
					clamp_variable = { var = debt min = 0 max = 1000000 }
					add_to_variable = { int_investments = TRK.int_investments }
					clamp_variable = { var = int_investments min = 0 max = 1000000 }
				}
				annex_country = { target = TRK transfer_troops = yes }
			}
			set_cosmetic_tag = CAS_united
			add_ideas = multi_ethnic_state_idea
			hidden_effect = {
				news_event = { id = kaz_cas.33 days = 2 }
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 1.5
		}
	}
	# Strengthen Confederation
	focus = {

		x = 1
		y = 8
		relative_position_id = KAZ_cas_central_asian_cooperation

		cost = 7

		prerequisite = { focus = KAZ_cas_confederate_treaty }
		mutually_exclusive = { focus = KAZ_cas_referendum_single_state }

		available = {
			is_subject = no
			NOT = { has_idea = non_power }
			NOT = { has_idea = minor_power }
			NOT = { has_government = nationalist }
			NOT = { has_government = fascism }
			is_faction_leader = yes
			if = {
				limit = {

						exists = yes
						is_in_faction_with = KAZ
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_war_with = UZB }
				UZB = { has_opinion = { target = KAZ value > 99 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					KYR = {
						exists = yes
						is_in_faction_with = KAZ
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_war_with = KYR }
				KYR = { has_opinion = { target = KAZ value > 99 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					TAJ = {
						exists = yes
						is_in_faction_with = KAZ
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_war_with = TAJ }
				TAJ = { has_opinion = { target = KAZ value > 99 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					TRK = {
						exists = yes
						is_in_faction_with = KAZ
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_war_with = TRK }
				TRK = { has_opinion = { target = KAZ value > 99 } }
			}
		}

		bypass = {
			NOT = { country_exists = UZB }
			NOT = { country_exists = KYR }
			NOT = { country_exists = TAJ }
			NOT = { country_exists = TRK }
		}

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus KAZ_cas_strengthen_confederation"
			every_faction_member = {
				limit = { has_idea = KAZ_cas_member_idea }
				set_country_flag = { flag = cas_reform_flag value = 1 days = 3 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 8 }
				change_domestic_influence_percentage = yes
				add_political_power = 150
				add_stability = 0.05
				add_to_tech_sharing_group = CAS_Tech_Share
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = KAZ_cas_member_idea
					add_idea = KAZ_cas_member_1_idea
				}
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 1.5
		}
	}
